`contribution(p,w)`:
We need conv p and reversed w. w length L=2n-1. Return length n.
```
L=w.size
wr=w[::-1]
N = 1 << ((n+L-2).bit_length())? Need N >= n+L-1 =3n-2. For n power2, 3n-2 -> N=4n (since next pow2 >3n). We can maybe use length 2N? But okay.

y=conv[n-1:3*n-2]? length? conv length >=3n-2. We need indices t=2n-2 down to n-1 inclusive length n. conv slice [n-1:2n-1] reversed? Earlier y_i=conv[2n-2-i], i=0..n-1 -> conv[2n-2 downto n-1]. This is conv[n-1:2n-1][::-1]. Yes length n.
```

      w=softmax(c-cmax,beta)
      h=contribution(p,w)
      hmax=h.max()
      if hmax==0 continue
      for sign in (-1, 1?) use negative primarily
        for eta in ...:
           q=log_update(p,h,eta,sign)
           s=score(q)
           if s<cur: cur=q=..., best=...
```

Softmax objective for full variables:
```
def full_softmax_objective(y, beta):
    n=len(y); y = y - np.max(y) to avoid overflow
    p=np.exp(y); p/=p.sum()
    F, grad_p = lse_score_grad_p(p,beta)
    gp_dot_p = np.dot(grad_p, p)
    grad_y = p * (grad_p - gp_dot_p)
    # add small penalty for y variance? maybe 1e-8 * sum y^2 /2
    return F+lam*np.dot(y,y), grad_y+2lam*y
```

If function internally subtracts max, the mathematical function is same softmax; gradient formula correct. Good. L-BFGS may see constant direction zero gradient; no issue.

    y = np.empty(n); y[:-1]=v; y[n-1]=0

A = rfft(p,Nconv)

Function:
```
def fft_self_conv_and_p(p, need_contrib=False):
    n=p.size; L=2*n-1
    if need_contrib: N=1 << (3*n-2 -1).bit_length()
    else: N=1 << (L-1).bit_length()
    P=np.fft.rfft(p,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(P*P,N)[:L]
    c=np.nan_to_num(c, nan=0,posinf=0,neginf=0); np.maximum(c,0,out=c)
    return c,P,N
```

Now `full_y_lse_objective(v, beta, reg)` top-level:
```
n = len(v)+1
y = np.empty(n); y[:-1]=v; y[-1]=0.0
# to avoid overflow, subtract max
ym = y - y.max()
p = np.exp(ym)
s = p.sum()
if not finite or s<=0: return 1e10, zeros(len(v))
p /= s
F, grad_p = lse_value_grad_p(p, beta)
gp_dot = np.dot(grad_p,p)
grad_y = p*(grad_p - gp_dot)
grad_v = grad_y[:-1]
obj = F
if reg>0: obj += 0.5*reg*dot(v,v); grad_v += reg*v
# clip grad
return obj, nan_to_num
```

Now log-mirror implementation details with lse_weights and P reuse.

```
def log_mirror_search(p0, deadline, max_outer=2000, rng=None, alphas=(1.0,2.0,5.0,10.0,20.0,50.0), etas=(0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5,1.0)):
    p=normalize(p0); cur=score_fast(p); best=p; best_sc=cur
    no_improve=0
    # maybe SA temp
    while time < deadline - 2:
        if no_improve>100:
            # perturb best
            p = perturb_log(best, rng, 0.1)
            cur = score_fast(p)
            no_improve=0
        c,P,N = fft_self_conv_and_p(p, True)
        cmax = max(c)
        if cmax<=0: break
        improved=False
        # choose alphas adaptively: start from medium; if current best near target, high
        for alpha in alphas:
            # weights with beta = alpha * n? Use cmax scale?
            beta = alpha * len(p)
            z = beta * (c - cmax)
            # z<=0; clip
            e=np.exp(np.clip(z, -50,0))
            s=e.sum()
            if s<=... continue
            w=e/s
            h=contrib_from_P(P,w,N,len(p))
            hmax=h.max()
            if hmax<=1e-300: continue
            # Try etas maybe sorted
            for eta in etas:
                q = log_update(p,h,eta)
                s = score_fast(q)
                if s < cur - 1e-14:
                    p=q; cur=s
                    if cur < best_sc:
                       best=p.copy(); best_sc=cur
                    improved=True
                    break
                # SA: accept if s < cur + temp? Need temp. If accept worse, cur=s; but then p worse might escape.
        if improved: no_improve=0
        else: no_improve +=1
    return best_sc,best
```
But using beta=alpha*n with alpha list. If p changes, cmax not used. For high alpha, weights active. Correct.

`log_update(p,h,eta)`:
```
logp=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300))
logq = logp - eta*(h/(hmax))  # hmax max

4. `fft_self_conv_and_p(p, need_contrib)`:
```
def fft_self_conv_and_p(p, need_contrib):
    p = np.asarray(p,float); n=p.size; L=2*n-1
    if need_contrib:
        M = 3*n - 2
    else:
        M = L
    N = 1 << (max(1,M)-1).bit_length()
    P = np.fft.rfft(p,N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(P*P,N)
    c = c[:L]
    c = np.nan_to_num(c, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    np.maximum(c,0.0,out=c)
    return c,P,N
```
For need_contrib, N may be 4n for power-of-two n; if n=8192, 3n-2=24574, next power 32768? Wait 2^15=32768; good. For n not power-of-two, N. For c-only M=L; N 2n.
5. `contrib_from_Pw(P,w,N,n)`:
```
def contrib_from_Pw(P,w,N,n):
    wr = w[::-1].copy()
    W = np.fft.rfft(wr,N)
    hfull = np.fft.irfft(P*W,N)
    h = hfull[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
    h = np.nan_to_num(h, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    np.maximum(h,0.0,out=h)
    return h
```
If hfull exactly length N; slice length n? n-1 to 2n-1 exclusive length n (2n-1 - (n-1)=n). Good.

def score_norm(p):

```

12. `run_y_lbfgs(p0,beta,deadline,maxfun,reg)`:
```
def run_y_lbfgs(p0, beta, deadline, maxfun=800, reg=1e-6):
   p0=normalize_a(p0)
   n=len(p0)
   y=np.log(np.maximum(p0,1e-300)); y-=np.max(y); y-=y[-1]
   v0=y[:-1]
   v0=np.nan_to_num(v0, nan=0.0)
   if len(v0)==0: return score_norm(p0), p0
   if time.monotonic() > deadline: return score_norm(p0),p0
   maxiter=max(30, maxfun//2)
   opts={'maxiter':maxiter,'maxfun':maxfun,'maxls':20,'ftol':1e-15,'gtol':1e-12,'disp':False}
   try:
      res=minimize(full_y_lse_objective, v0, args=(beta,reg), jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', options=opts)
      v=res.x if res.x is not None else v0
   except Exception:
      v=v0
   v=np.nan_to_num(v, nan=0.0, posinf=50.0, neginf=-50.0)
   p=softmax_from_v(v)
   sc=score_norm(p)
   return sc,p
```
Need `softmax_from_v(v)`:
```
y=np.empty(n); y[:-1]=v; y[-1]=0; y-=y.max(); p=exp(y); s=p.sum(); if s<=0 uniform; return p/s
```
Top-level.
Bounds? None. But L-BFGS-B without bounds works (method L-BFGS-B can handle unbounded). `reg` optional.

```

A=np.dot(gp,p)

meanB=B.T.dot(p)
Bgp=B.T.dot(gp_p)
grad=Bgp - meanB*A
obj=F

return obj, np.nan_to_num(grad)
```

def perturb_log(p, rng, scale=0.1):
   p=normalize_a(p); n=p.size
   logp=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300))
   noise=rng.standard_normal(n)
   k=max(3, int(n//500))
   if k%2==0:k+=1
   if k < n:
      c=k//2; sig=max(1.0,c/2.0)
      kern=np.exp(-((np.arange(k)-c)**2)/(2*sig*sig)); kern/=kern.sum()
      noise=np.convolve(noise,kern,mode='same')
   noise-=noise.mean()
   std=noise.std()
   if std>0: noise=noise/std
   logq=logp+scale*noise
   logq-=logq.max()
   q=np.exp(logq); q=np.nan_to_num...
   return normalize_a(q)
```

Let's implement `top_candidates(cands, n_rank, k)`:

```
def select_top_candidates(cands, n_rank, k):
    selected=[]
    for p in cands:
       pr=normalize_a(resample_sequence(p,n_rank))
       sc=score_norm(pr)
       if not finite: continue
       # insert sorted
       pos=len(selected)
       for i in range(len(selected)):
          if sc < selected[i][0]: pos=i; break
       if pos < len(selected): selected.insert(pos,(sc,pr))
       else: selected.append((sc,pr))
       if len(selected)>k: del selected[k:]
    # trim
    return [item[1] for item in selected[:k]]
```

`normalize_a`:
```
def normalize_a(a, target=1.0):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a=np.nan_to_num(a,nan=0.0,posinf=1e6,neginf=0.0)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,None)
    S=float(a.sum())
    if S < 1e-15:
        return np.full(a.size,float(target)/max(1,a.size),dtype=np.float64)
    return a*(float(target)/S)
```
If a.size=0, returns empty? `max(1,a.size)`. But empty -> size 0. We can if size==0 return empty. But candidate never empty. Add `if a.size==0: return np.array([],dtype=np.float64)`.

`resample_sequence`:
```
def resample_sequence(a,n):
    a=normalize_a? No, just array; input may not normalized. Use `a=np.asarray(a,float); a=np.nan_to_num...; a=np.clip(a,0,None)`
    n=int(n); m=a.size
    if m==0: return np.ones(n,dtype=float)
    if m==n: return a.copy()
    x_old=np.linspace(0,1,m); x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    return np.interp(x_new,x_old,a).astype(float)
```

`fft_self_conv_and_p` if p size 0: return empty, empty? But p not empty. For n=1, M=1.
```
def fft_self_conv_and_p(p, need_contrib):
    p=np.asarray(p,float).ravel()
    n=p.size
    if n==0: return np.empty(0), np.empty(0), 1
    L=2*n-1
    if need_contrib:
       M=3*n-2
    else:
       M=L
    if M < 1: M=1
    N=1 << (M-1).bit_length()
    P=np.fft.rfft(p,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(P*P,N)[:L]
    c=np.nan_to_num(c, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    if c.size:
       np.maximum(c,0.0,out=c)
    return c,P,N
```
For need_contrib, if n=1 M=1 N=1. Good.

`contrib_from_Pw`:
```
def contrib_from_Pw(P,w,N,n):
    if n==0: return np.empty(0)
    wr=np.asarray(w,float)[::-1].copy()
    W=np.fft.rfft(wr,N)
    h=np.fft.irfft(P*W,N)
    h=h[n-1:2*n-1]
    h=h[::-1]
    h=np.nan_to_num(h,...)
    if h.size: np.maximum(h,0.0,out=h)
    return h
```
If slice length shorter due N? N>=3n-2 ensures. Good.

`score_norm`:
```
def score_norm(p):
    p=np.asarray(p,float).ravel()
    if p.size==0: return np.inf
    c,_,_=fft_self_conv_and_p(p,False)
    if c.size==0: return np.inf
    return float(2.0*p.size*np.max(c))
```
`score_fast`: normalize then score_norm.

`lse_value_grad_p`:
```
def lse_value_grad_p(p, beta):
    p=np.asarray(p,float).ravel()
    n=p.size
    S=float(p.sum())
    if n==0 or S < 1e-15: return 1e10, np.zeros(n)
    pc = p if abs(S-1.0) <= 1e-12 else p / S
    c,P,N=fft_self_conv_and_p(pc, True)
    if c.size==0: return 1e10, np.zeros(n)
    cmax=float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
       return 2.0*n*0.0, np.zeros(n)
    beta=float(beta)
    if beta <= 1e-12:
       w=np.ones(c.size)/c.size
       logZ=float(np.log(c.size))
    else:
       z=beta*(c-cmax)
       z=np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=-50.0)
       # shift by max for stability
       zmax=float(np.max(z)) if z.size else 0.0
       z=z-zmax
       z=np.clip(z, -50.0, 50.0)
       e=np.exp(z)
       Z=float(e.sum())
       if not np.isfinite(Z) or Z <= 0.0:
          w=np.ones(c.size)/c.size
          logZ=float(np.log(c.size))
       else:
          w=e/Z
          logZ=float(np.log(Z) + zmax)
    h=contrib_from_Pw(P,w,N,n)
    G=cmax + logZ/beta if beta>0 else cmax
    F=2.0*n*G
    grad=4.0*n*h
    grad=np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return float(F), grad
```
If beta=0 not used. If cmax small but >0. Good. Note if zmax not zero due cmax not max? Should be 0. logZ includes zmax. Since zmax≈0, okay.

`softmax_from_v`:

def softmax_from_v(v):
   v=np.asarray(v,float).ravel()
   n=len(v)+1
   y=np.empty(n,float)
   y[:-1]=v; y[-1]=0.0
   y=np.nan_to_num(y, nan=0.0, posinf=50.0, neginf=-50.0)
   y-=np.max(y)
   p=np.exp(y)
   s=float(p.sum())
   if not finite or s<=0: p=np.ones(n)/n
   else p/=s
   return p
```
Maybe clip y to [-700,700] before exp to avoid overflow after subtract max: after subtract max max=0, values <=0, no overflow, underflow okay. nan_to_num before subtract. If y all -inf? nan_to_num sets 0? okay.

`full_y_lse_objective(v,beta,reg)`:
```
def full_y_lse_objective(v,beta,reg):
   v=np.asarray(v,float).ravel()
   n=len(v)
   if n==0:
      return 1e10, np.zeros(0)
   p=softmax_from_v(v)
   F,gp=lse_value_grad_p(p,beta)
   if not np.isfinite(F):
      return 1e10, np.zeros(n)
   A=float(np.dot(gp,p))
   grady = p*(gp - A)
   gradv = grady[:-1]
   obj=F
   if reg and reg>0:
      obj += 0.5*reg*float(np.dot(v,v))
      gradv = gradv + reg*v
   gradv=np.nan_to_num(gradv, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
   # project? not needed
   return float(obj), gradv
```
If v length n-1, n_var. `n` in objective variable `n=len(v)` but p length n+1. To avoid confusion:
```
m = len(v)
p_len = m+1
p = softmax_from_v(v)
...
return gradv length m
```
Use m.

def run_y_lbfgs(p0,beta,deadline,maxfun=800,reg=1e-6):
   p=normalize_a(p0)
   if p.size <=1: return score_norm(p),p
   if p.size>1 and p[0] > p[-1]: p=p[::-1]
   y=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300))
   y=np.nan_to_num(y,nan=-700.0,posinf=700.0,neginf=-700.0)

   y-=y[-1]
   v0=y[:-1].copy()
   v0=np.nan_to_num(v0,nan=0.0,posinf=50.0,neginf=-50.0)
   maxfun=int(max(50,min(int(maxfun), ...)))
   if time.monotonic() >= deadline: return score_norm(p),p
   opts=... maxiter=int(max(20,maxfun//2))
   try: res=minimize(...)
   except: res=None
   if res is not None and res.x is not None: v=res.x else v=v0

   # Perhaps if p2[0] > p2[-1]? don't reverse after because objective fixed; but sequence score same; no need.

```
def make_basis(n, M):
    n=int(n); M=int(M)
    if n <=0: return np.zeros((0,0))
    if M <=0: M=1
    if M > n: M=n
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    B=np.zeros((n,M),float)
    j=0
    if j<M: B[:,j]=x-0.5; j+=1
    if j<M: B[:,j]=x*x - 1.0/3.0; j+=1
    if j<M: B[:,j]=(x-0.5)**3; j+=1
    if j<M: B[:,j]=x**4 - 0.25; j+=1  # mean x^4=1/5? Wait ∫x^4=1/5, zero mean 1/5. Use 0.2 no. Let's use (x**2 -0.5)**2? mean 1/12? Simpler: B=x**3 - 0.25 (mean x^3=1/4). Use for 4th? Already cubic. col3: x**3 - 0.25.
```

`estimate_eval_time(n)`: We'll set based on N.
```
def estimate_eval_time(n):
   N = 1 << (max(1, 3*n-3)-1).bit_length()
   return max(0.001, 1.5e-5 * N)
```

`log_mirror_search` code details:
```
def log_mirror_search(p0, deadline, rng=None, alpha_values=(0.5,1.0,3.0,10.0,30.0), eta_values=(0.1,0.3,1.0), max_outer=2000):
    p=normalize_a(p0)
    if rng is None: rng=np.random.default_rng(12345)
    best=p.copy(); best_sc=score_norm(p)
    cur=p.copy(); cur_sc=best_sc
    n=len(p)
    no_improve=0
    outer=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline and outer < max_outer:
       outer += 1
       c,P,N = fft_self_conv_and_p(cur, True)
       cmax=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
       if cmax <= 0 or not finite: break
       improved=False
       # choose alpha_values maybe up to 4
       for alpha in alpha_values:
           beta=alpha*float(n)
           z=beta*(c-cmax)
           z=np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=-50.0)
           zmax=float(np.max(z)) if z.size else 0.0
           z=z-zmax
           e=np.exp(np.clip(z,-50,50))
           Z=float(e.sum())
           if Z<=0 or not finite: continue
           w=e/Z
           h=contrib_from_Pw(P,w,N,n)
           hmax=float(np.max(h))
           if hmax <= 1e-300: continue
           ratio=h/hmax
           for eta in eta_values:
               if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
               #试 negative update
               logp=np.log(np.maximum(cur,1e-300))
               logq=logp - eta*ratio
               logq-=np.max(logq)
               q=np.exp(logq)
               q=np.nan_to_num(q,nan=0.0,posinf=0.0,neginf=0.0)
               q=np.clip(q,0,None)
               Sq=float(q.sum())
               if Sq <=1e-300: q=cur.copy()
               else: q=q/Sq
               sc=score_norm(q)
               if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < cur_sc - 1e-14:
                   cur=q.copy(); cur_sc=sc
                   if sc < best_sc:
                       best=q.copy(); best_sc=sc
                   improved=True
                   break
               # maybe try positive small? no
               # If SA: if sc < cur_sc + 1e-5 and rng.random()<... accept? Let's skip.
       if improved:
           no_improve=0
       else:
           no_improve += 1
           if no_improve > 40:
               p_new = perturb_log(best, rng, scale=0.15)
               sc = score_norm(p_new)
               if not finite: sc=cur_sc
               cur=p_new.copy(); cur_sc=sc
               no_improve=0
    return best_sc, best
```

Better if no improve in an outer, perturb immediately (or use randomization). Change weights? We can vary alpha/eta but no improve. If no improve, do a small perturb to cur from cur or best. Let's set no_improve threshold 0: if not improved: after maybe 5, perturb. But if perturbation improves, fine. We can perturb with small scale and set cur to perturbed (even if worse) to explore. This ensures progress. But if perturb makes score much worse, subsequent updates may not recover? We keep best. Could be okay. We'll perturb every 10 stagnation.

`refine_candidate` code:
```
def refine_candidate(p0,n,deadline,label,rng):
    p=normalize_a(resample_sequence(p0,n))
    best=p.copy(); best_sc=score_norm(p)
    print(label,"start",best_sc,time)
    start=time.monotonic()
    total=max(20.0, deadline-start)
    # y stage until 65%
    y_end = start + 0.65*total
    alphas=(2.0,8.0,30.0)
    # allocate y_end equally
    for i,alpha in enumerate(alphas):
       sub_end = start + ((i+1)* (y_end-start) / len(alphas))
       rem = sub_end - time.monotonic()
       if rem < 15: break
       maxfun = int(min(1200, max(200, rem*0.7 / max(0.001, estimate_eval_time(n)))))
       sc,p2=run_y_lbfgs(best, alpha*float(n), sub_end, maxfun, 1e-6)
       if finite and sc<best_sc:
          best_sc=sc; best=p2.copy(); print
       # quick log mirror until 20s before sub_end
       lm_end = min(sub_end, max(time.monotonic()+5, time.monotonic()+min(20.0, 0.2*(sub_end-time.monotonic()))))
       if time.monotonic() < lm_end:
          sc,p2=log_mirror_search(best, lm_end, rng, alpha_values=(0.5,2.0,8.0), eta_values=(0.1,0.3,0.8), max_outer=300)
          if sc<best_sc: update
    # final log mirror
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 5:
       sc,p2=log_mirror_search(best, deadline-5, rng, alpha_values=(0.5,1,3,10), eta_values=(0.1,0.3,0.8,1.5), max_outer=3000)
       if update
    return best_sc,best
```
Need not overcomplicate. The quick log mirror in each stage may be too much. But okay.

Let's write manual insertion function:
```
def insert_top(items, score, payload, k):
    # items list of [score,payload]
    pos = len(items)
    for i in range(len(items)):
        if score < items[i][0]:
            pos = i
            break
    if pos < len(items):
        items.insert(pos, (score, payload))
    else:
        items.append((score, payload))
    if len(items) > k:
        del items[k:]
    return items
```
Top-level. `select_top_candidates` uses.

`select_top_candidates`:
```
def select_top_candidates(cands, n_rank, k):
    if n_rank <=0 or k<=0: return []
    top=[]
    for p in cands:
        p=np.asarray(p,float).ravel()
        if p.size==0: continue
        pr=normalize_a(resample_sequence(p,n_rank))
        sc=score_norm(pr)
        if not np.isfinite(sc): continue
        top=insert_top(top, sc, pr, k)
    return [payload for score,payload in top]
```
Order top by score increasing. If scores close no issue.

Define top-level `clean_nonnegative(arr)`:
```
def clean_nonnegative(arr):
    arr=np.asarray(arr,float).ravel()
    arr=np.nan_to_num(arr,...)
    arr=np.clip(arr,0,None)
    if arr.size==0: return np.ones(1)
    if arr.sum()<=0: arr=np.ones(arr.size)
    return normalize_a(arr)
```

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step coefficients minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / sum(a)^2
using continuous/log-domain mirror descent, low-dimensional Fourier/polynomial
basis optimization, low-resolution full variable LSE refinement, and a final
high-resolution active-set polishing phase.
"""

import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def normalize_a(a, target=1.0):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-15:
        return np.full(a.size, float(target) / float(a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return a * (float(target) / s)


def clean_nonnegative(arr):
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, None)
    if arr.sum() <= 0:
        arr = np.ones(arr.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return normalize_a(arr)


def resample_sequence(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    n = int(n)
    m = int(a.size)
    if m == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if m == n:
        return a.copy()
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    return np.interp(x_new, x_old, a).astype(np.float64)


def fft_self_conv_and_p(p, need_contrib):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(p.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64), np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64), 1
    L = 2 * n - 1
    if need_contrib:
        M = 3 * n - 2
    else:
        M = L
    if M < 1:
        M = 1
    N = 1 << (M - 1).bit_length()
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(P * P, N)[:L]
    c = np.nan_to_num(c, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    if c.size:
        np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    return c, P, N


def contrib_from_Pw(P, w, N, n):
    n = int(n)
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    wr = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)[::-1].copy()
    W = np.fft.rfft(wr, N)
    h = np.fft.irfft(P * W, N)
    h = h[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    h = h[::-1]
    h = np.nan_to_num(h, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    if h.size:
        np.maximum(h, 0.0, out=h)
    return h


def score_norm(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if p.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    c, _, _ = fft_self_conv_and_p(p, False)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * p.size * np.max(c))


def score_fast(a):
    a = normalize_a(a)
    return score_norm(a)


def lse_value_grad_p(p, beta):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(p.size)
    if n == 0:
        return 1e10, np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    S = float(p.sum())
    if S < 1e-15:
        return 1e10, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if abs(S - 1.0) <= 1e-12:
        pc = p
    else:
        pc = p / S
    c, P, N = fft_self_conv_and_p(pc, True)
    if c.size == 0:
        return 1e10, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
        return 0.0, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    beta = float(beta)
    if beta <= 1e-12:
        w = np.ones(c.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(c.size)
        logZ = float(np.log(c.size))
    else:
        z = beta * (c - cmax)
        z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=-50.0)
        zmax = float(np.max(z)) if z.size else 0.0
        z = z - zmax
        z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 50.0)
        e = np.exp(z)
        Z = float(e.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(Z) or Z <= 0.0:
            w = np.ones(c.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(c.size)
            logZ = float(np.log(c.size))
        else:
            w = e / Z
            logZ = float(np.log(Z) + zmax)
    h = contrib_from_Pw(P, w, N, n)
    G = cmax + logZ / beta
    F = 2.0 * float(n) * G
    grad = 4.0 * float(n) * h
    grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return float(F), grad


def softmax_from_v(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    v = np.nan_to_num(v, nan=0.0, posinf=100.0, neginf=-100.0)
    v = np.clip(v, -100.0, 100.0)
    n = int(v.size) + 1
    y = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    y[:-1] = v
    y[-1] = 0.0
    y = y - np.max(y)
    p = np.exp(y)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    else:
        p = p / s
    return p


def full_y_lse_objective(v, beta, reg):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    m = int(v.size)
    if m == 0:
        return 1e10, np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.nan_to_num(v, nan=0.0, posinf=100.0, neginf=-100.0)
    v = np.clip(v, -100.0, 100.0)
    p = softmax_from_v(v)
    F, gp = lse_value_grad_p(p, beta)
    if not np.isfinite(F):
        return 1e10, np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    A = float(np.dot(gp, p))
    gradv = p * (gp - A)
    gradv = gradv[:-1]
    obj = float(F)
    if reg is not None and float(reg) > 0.0:
        reg = float(reg)
        obj += 0.5 * reg * float(np.dot(v, v))
        gradv = gradv + reg * v
    gradv = np.nan_to_num(gradv, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return obj, gradv


def run_y_lbfgs(p0, beta, deadline, maxfun=800, reg=1e-6):
    p = normalize_a(p0)
    if p.size <= 1:
        return score_norm(p), p
    if p.size > 1 and p[0] > p[-1]:
        p = p[::-1]
    y = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
    y = np.nan_to_num(y, nan=-700.0, posinf=700.0, neginf=-700.0)
    y = y - np.max(y)
    y = y - y[-1]
    v0 = y[:-1].copy()
    v0 = np.nan_to_num(v0, nan=0.0, posinf=50.0, neginf=-50.0)
    v0 = np.clip(v0, -50.0, 50.0)

    if time.monotonic() >= float(deadline):
        return score_norm(p), p

    maxfun = int(max(50, min(int(maxfun), 2500)))
    opts = {
        "ftol": 1e-15,
        "gtol": 1e-10,
        "maxiter": int(max(30, maxfun // 2)),
        "maxfun": maxfun,
        "maxls": 30,
        "disp": False,
    }
    try:
        res = minimize(
            full_y_lse_objective,
            v0,
            args=(float(beta), float(reg)),
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            options=opts,
        )
        if getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
            v = res.x
        else:
            v = v0
    except Exception:
        v = v0

    v = np.nan_to_num(v, nan=0.0, posinf=100.0, neginf=-100.0)
    v = np.clip(v, -100.0, 100.0)
    p2 = softmax_from_v(v)
    sc = score_norm(p2)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        p2 = p
        sc = score_norm(p)
    return float(sc), p2


def make_basis(n, M):
    n = int(n)
    M = int(M)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.zeros((0, 0), dtype=np.float64)
    if M <= 0:
        M = 1
    if M > n:
        M = n
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    B = np.zeros((n, M), dtype=np.float64)
    j = 0
    if j < M:
        B[:, j] = x - 0.5
        j += 1
    if j < M:
        B[:, j] = x * x - 1.0 / 3.0
        j += 1
    if j < M:
        B[:, j] = x * x * x - 0.25
        j += 1
    if j < M:
        B[:, j] = x * (1.0 - x) - 1.0 / 6.0
        j += 1
    m = 1
    while j < M:
        B[:, j] = np.cos(np.pi * m * x)
        j += 1
        if j < M:
            B[:, j] = np.sin(np.pi * m * x)
            j += 1
        m += 1
    for jj in range(M):
        col = B[:, jj].copy()
        col = col - col.mean()
        std = float(col.std())
        if std < 1e-12:
            col = np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * jj * x)
            col = col - col.mean()
            std = float(col.std())
        if std > 1e-12:
            col = col / std
        else:
            col = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        B[:, jj] = col
    return B


def sequence_from_basis(theta, B):
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    B = np.asarray(B, dtype=np.float64)
    if B.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    theta = np.nan_to_num(theta, nan=0.0, posinf=50.0, neginf=-50.0)
    theta = np.clip(theta, -100.0, 100.0)
    z = B.dot(theta)
    z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=200.0, neginf=-200.0)
    z = np.clip(z, -200.0, 200.0)
    z = z - np.max(z)
    p = np.exp(z)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        p = np.ones(B.shape[0], dtype=np.float64) / float(B.shape[0])
    else:
        p = p / s
    return normalize_a(p)


def theta_lse_objective(theta, beta, B, reg):
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    M = int(B.shape[1]) if B.ndim == 2 else 0
    if M == 0:
        return 1e10, np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    theta = np.nan_to_num(theta, nan=0.0, posinf=100.0, neginf=-100.0)
    theta = np.clip(theta, -100.0, 100.0)
    p = sequence_from_basis(theta, B)
    F, gp = lse_value_grad_p(p, beta)
    if not np.isfinite(F):
        return 1e10, np.zeros(M, dtype=np.float64)
    A = float(np.dot(gp, p))
    gp_p = gp * p
    meanB = B.T.dot(p)
    Bgp = B.T.dot(gp_p)
    grad = Bgp - meanB * A
    obj = float(F)
    if reg is not None and float(reg) > 0.0:
        reg = float(reg)
        obj += 0.5 * reg * float(np.dot(theta, theta))
        grad = grad + reg * theta
    grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return obj, grad


def run_basis_lbfgs(B, beta, theta0, maxfun=400, reg=1e-5, deadline=None):
    theta = np.asarray(theta0, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    M = int(B.shape[1]) if B.ndim == 2 else 0
    if M == 0:
        return theta
    theta = np.nan_to_num(theta, nan=0.0, posinf=100.0, neginf=-100.0)
    theta = np.clip(theta, -100.0, 100.0)
    if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() >= float(deadline):
        return theta
    maxfun = int(max(32, min(int(maxfun), 2000)))
    opts = {
        "ftol": 1e-15,
        "gtol": 1e-10,
        "maxiter": int(max(20, maxfun // 2)),
        "maxfun": maxfun,
        "maxls": 30,
        "disp": False,
    }
    try:
        res = minimize(
            theta_lse_objective,
            theta,
            args=(float(beta), B, float(reg)),
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            options=opts,
        )
        if getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
            theta = res.x
    except Exception:
        pass
    theta = np.nan_to_num(theta, nan=0.0, posinf=100.0, neginf=-100.0)
    return np.clip(theta, -100.0, 100.0)


def project_theta_from_seq(p, B):
    p = normalize_a(p)
    if B.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    logp = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
    logp = logp - float(logp.mean())
    try:
        theta = np.linalg.lstsq(B, logp, rcond=None)[0]
    except Exception:
        theta = np.zeros(B.shape[1], dtype=np.float64)
    theta = np.nan_to_num(theta, nan=0.0, posinf=50.0, neginf=-50.0)
    return np.clip(theta, -50.0, 50.0)


def make_inits(n, rng, parent=None):
    n = int(n)
    inits = []
    if n <= 0:
        return inits
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)

    inits.append(clean_nonnegative(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)))

    if parent is not None:
        pr = resample_sequence(parent, n)
        inits.append(clean_nonnegative(pr))
        inits.append(clean_nonnegative(pr[::-1]))

    for a in (-1.5, -0.8, -0.3, 0.3, 0.8, 1.5):
        inits.append(clean_nonnegative(1.0 + a * x))

    for a in (-1.5, -0.7, 0.7, 1.5):
        inits.append(clean_nonnegative(1.0 + a * (x - 0.5) ** 2))

    for r in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
        inits.append(clean_nonnegative(np.exp(r * x)))
        inits.append(clean_nonnegative(np.exp(-r * x)))
        inits.append(clean_nonnegative(np.exp(r * (x - 0.5))))

    eps = 1e-6
    inits.append(clean_nonnegative(1.0 / (x + eps)))
    inits.append(clean_nonnegative(1.0 / (1.0 - x + eps)))
    inits.append(clean_nonnegative(np.sqrt(1.0 / ((x + eps) * (1.0 - x + eps)))))

    for a in (-1.0, -0.3, 0.3, 1.0):
        for b in (-1.0, -0.3, 0.3, 1.0):
            arr = (x + eps) ** a * (1.0 - x + eps) ** b
            inits.append(clean_nonnegative(arr))
            if len(inits) > 70:
                break
        if len(inits) > 70:
            break

    for a in (-1.0, -0.6, -0.2, 0.2, 0.6, 1.0):
        inits.append(clean_nonnegative(1.0 + a * np.cos(np.pi * x)))
        inits.append(clean_nonnegative(1.0 + a * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x)))
        inits.append(clean_nonnegative(1.0 + a * np.sin(np.pi * x)))
        if len(inits) > 100:
            break

    for _ in range(6):
        noise = rng.standard_normal(n)
        k = max(3, int(n // 300))
        if k % 2 == 0:
            k += 1
        if k < n:
            c = k // 2
            sig = max(1.0, c / 2.0)
            kern = np.exp(-((np.arange(k) - c) ** 2) / (2.0 * sig * sig))
            kern = kern / float(kern.sum())
            noise = np.convolve(noise, kern, mode="same")
        noise = noise - float(noise.mean())
        std = float(noise.std())
        if std > 0:
            noise = noise / std
        inits.append(clean_nonnegative(1.0 + 0.8 * noise))

    return inits[:100]


def estimate_eval_time(n):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    M = 3 * n - 2
    N = 1 << (max(1, M) - 1).bit_length()
    return max(0.0005, 2.0e-7 * float(N))


def insert_top(items, score, payload, k):
    pos = len(items)
    for i in range(len(items)):
        if score < items[i][0]:
            pos = i
            break
    if pos < len(items):
        items.insert(pos, (score, payload))
    else:
        items.append((score, payload))
    if len(items) > k:
        del items[k:]
    return items


def select_top_candidates(cands, n_rank, k):
    n_rank = int(n_rank)
    k = int(k)
    if n_rank <= 0 or k <= 0:
        return []
    top = []
    for p in cands:
        p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if p.size == 0:
            continue
        pr = normalize_a(resample_sequence(p, n_rank))
        sc = score_norm(pr)
        if not np.isfinite(sc):
            continue
        top = insert_top(top, float(sc), pr, k)
    return [x[1] for x in top]


def perturb_log(p, rng, scale=0.1):
    p = normalize_a(p)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n == 0:
        return p
    logp = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
    noise = rng.standard_normal(n)
    k = max(3, int(n // 500))
    if k % 2 == 0:
        k += 1
    if k < n:
        c = k // 2
        sig = max(1.0, c / 2.0)
        kern = np.exp(-((np.arange(k) - c) ** 2) / (2.0 * sig * sig))
        kern = kern / float(kern.sum())
        noise = np.convolve(noise, kern, mode="same")
    noise = noise - float(noise.mean())
    std = float(noise.std())
    if std > 0:
        noise = noise / std
    logq = logp + float(scale) * noise
    logq = logq - np.max(logq)
    q = np.exp(logq)
    q = np.nan_to_num(q, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    q = np.clip(q, 0.0, None)
    return normalize_a(q)


def log_mirror_search(p0, deadline, rng=None, alpha_values=None, eta_values=None, max_outer=3000):
    p = normalize_a(p0)
    if p.size == 0:
        return np.inf, p
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(987654)
    if alpha_values is None:
        alpha_values = (0.5, 1.0, 3.0, 10.0, 30.0)
    if eta_values is None:
        eta_values = (0.1, 0.3, 1.0)
    best = p.copy()
    best_sc = score_norm(p)
    cur = p.copy()
    cur_sc = best_sc
    n = len(p)
    no_improve = 0
    outer = 0
    while time.monotonic() < float(deadline) and outer < int(max_outer):
        outer += 1
        if cur_sc > best_sc + 0.05:
            cur = best.copy()
            cur_sc = best_sc
        c, P, N = fft_self_conv_and_p(cur, True)
        if c.size == 0:
            break
        cmax = float(np.max(c))
        if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
            break
        improved = False
        for alpha in alpha_values:
            beta = float(alpha) * float(n)
            z = beta * (c - cmax)
            z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=-50.0)
            zmax = float(np.max(z)) if z.size else 0.0
            z = z - zmax
            e = np.exp(np.clip(z, -50.0, 50.0))
            Z = float(e.sum())
            if not np.isfinite(Z) or Z <= 0.0:
                continue
            w = e / Z
            h = contrib_from_Pw(P, w, N, n)
            hmax = float(np.max(h))
            if not np.isfinite(hmax) or hmax <= 1e-300:
                continue
            ratio = h / hmax
            for eta in eta_values:
                if time.monotonic() >= float(deadline):
                    break
                logp = np.log(np.maximum(cur, 1e-300))
                logq = logp - float(eta) * ratio
                logq = logq - np.max(logq)
                q = np.exp(logq)
                q = np.nan_to_num(q, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
                q = np.clip(q, 0.0, None)
                s = float(q.sum())
                if s <= 1e-300:
                    q = cur.copy()
                else:
                    q = q / s
                sc = score_norm(q)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < cur_sc - 1e-14:
                    cur = q.copy()
                    cur_sc = float(sc)
                    if sc < best_sc:
                        best = q.copy()
                        best_sc = float(sc)
                    improved = True
                    break
            if improved:
                break
        if improved:
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve >= 3:
                scale = 0.05 + 0.04 * min(5.0, float(no_improve) / 3.0)
                cur = perturb_log(best, rng, scale)
                cur_sc = score_norm(cur)
                if not np.isfinite(cur_sc):
                    cur = best.copy()
                    cur_sc = best_sc
                no_improve = 0
    return float(best_sc), best


def refine_candidate(p0, n, deadline, label, rng):
    p = normalize_a(resample_sequence(p0, n))
    if p.size == 0:
        p = np.ones(int(n), dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    best = p.copy()
    best_sc = score_norm(p)
    print(label, "start", float(best_sc), "time", time.monotonic())

    start = time.monotonic()
    total = max(30.0, float(deadline) - start)
    y_end = start + 0.62 * total
    alphas = (2.0, 8.0, 30.0)

    for i, alpha in enumerate(alphas):
        sub_end = start + float(i + 1) * (y_end - start) / float(len(alphas))
        rem = float(sub_end - time.monotonic())
        if rem < 12.0:
            break
        maxfun = int(min(1800, max(160, rem * 0.75 / max(0.001, estimate_eval_time(n)))))
        sc, p2 = run_y_lbfgs(best, float(alpha) * float(n), sub_end, maxfun, 1e-6)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best = p2.copy()
            best_sc = float(sc)
            print(label, "y-lbfgs beta=", float(alpha * n), "score", float(best_sc))
        lm_end = min(float(sub_end), float(time.monotonic() + min(30.0, 0.18 * max(5.0, sub_end - time.monotonic()))))
        if time.monotonic() < lm_end:
            sc, p2 = log_mirror_search(
                best,
                lm_end,
                rng,
                alpha_values=(0.5, 2.0, 8.0),
                eta_values=(0.1, 0.3, 0.8),
                max_outer=400,
            )
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best = p2.copy()
                best_sc = float(sc)
                print(label, "mirror score", float(best_sc))

    if time.monotonic() < float(deadline) - 5.0:
        sc, p2 = log_mirror_search(
            best,
            float(deadline) - 5.0,
            rng,
            alpha_values=(0.5, 1.0, 3.0, 10.0, 30.0),
            eta_values=(0.1, 0.3, 0.8, 1.5),
            max_outer=5000,
        )
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best = p2.copy()
            best_sc = float(sc)
            print(label, "final mirror score", float(best_sc))

    return float(best_sc), best


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 2400.0
    if budget <= 0:
        budget = 2400.0
    reserve = max(90.0, 0.05 * budget)
    deadline = t_start + max(60.0, budget - reserve)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) + 1234567)

    parent = None
    try:
        h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if h is not None:
            pp = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            pp = np.nan_to_num(pp, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
            pp = np.clip(pp, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if pp.size >= 32 and float(pp.sum()) > 0.0:
                parent = normalize_a(pp)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    print("parent available:", parent is not None, "len", parent.size if parent is not None else 0)

    total = max(60.0, deadline - t_start)
    t_basis_end = t_start + min(420.0, 0.18 * total)
    t_lowfull_end = t_start + min(760.0, 0.32 * total)
    t_high8_end = t_start + min(1700.0, 0.72 * total)
    t_high16_end = t_start + min(2100.0, 0.88 * total)
    final_dead = deadline - 45.0

    low_cands = []
    n_b = 2048

    if parent is not None:
        ppar = normalize_a(resample_sequence(parent, n_b))
        low_cands.append(ppar)

    B = make_basis(n_b, 12)
    inits = make_inits(n_b, rng, parent)
    inits_top = select_top_candidates(inits, n_b, 8)

    theta_starts = []
    theta_starts.append(np.zeros(12, dtype=np.float64))
    theta_starts.append(rng.normal(scale=1.0, size=12).astype(np.float64))
    theta_starts.append(rng.normal(scale=2.5, size=12).astype(np.float64))
    if inits_top:
        theta_starts.append(project_theta_from_seq(inits_top[0], B))
    if parent is not None:
        theta_starts.append(project_theta_from_seq(normalize_a(resample_sequence(parent, n_b)), B))

    beta_values = (float(n_b), 4.0 * float(n_b), 16.0 * float(n_b), 64.0 * float(n_b))
    for th0 in theta_starts:
        theta = np.asarray(th0, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if theta.size != B.shape[1]:
            theta = np.zeros(B.shape[1], dtype=np.float64)
        for beta in beta_values:
            if time.monotonic() > float(t_basis_end) - 5.0:
                break
            theta = run_basis_lbfgs(B, beta, theta, 420, 1e-5, t_basis_end)
            p = normalize_a(sequence_from_basis(theta, B))
            low_cands.append(p)
            sc = score_norm(p)
            print("basis beta", beta, "n=", n_b, "score", float(sc) if np.isfinite(sc) else "inf")

    pool = low_cands + inits_top
    if parent is not None:
        pool.append(normalize_a(resample_sequence(parent, n_b)))
    full_starts = select_top_candidates(pool, 1024, 3)
    n_f = 1024
    for p_start in full_starts:
        p = normalize_a(resample_sequence(p_start, n_f))
        for beta in (2.0 * float(n_f), 8.0 * float(n_f), 32.0 * float(n_f)):
            if time.monotonic() > float(t_lowfull_end) - 10.0:
                break
            sc, p = run_y_lbfgs(p, beta, t_lowfull_end, 1200, 1e-6)
            print("lowfull beta", beta, "n=", n_f, "score", float(sc) if np.isfinite(sc) else "inf")
        low_cands.append(normalize_a(p))

    pool = low_cands + inits_top
    if parent is not None:
        pool.append(normalize_a(resample_sequence(parent, n_b)))
    high_starts = select_top_candidates(pool, 2048, 3)
    if not high_starts:
        high_starts = [np.ones(2048, dtype=np.float64) / 2048.0]

    global_best = None
    global_sc = np.inf

    n8 = 8192
    high_start = time.monotonic()
    high_dur = max(30.0, float(t_high8_end) - high_start)
    cum = 0.0
    for idx, ps in enumerate(high_starts[:3]):
        frac = (0.50, 0.35, 0.15)[idx]
        cum += frac
        sub_dead = min(float(t_high8_end), high_start + cum * high_dur)
        if float(sub_dead) - time.monotonic() < 30.0:
            break
        sc, p8 = refine_candidate(ps, n8, sub_dead, "high8-%d" % idx, rng)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < global_sc:
            global_sc = float(sc)
            global_best = p8.copy()
            print("high8-%d kept score" % idx, float(global_sc))

    if global_best is None:
        global_best = normalize_a(resample_sequence(high_starts[0], n8))
        global_sc = score_norm(global_best)
        print("fallback high8 score", float(global_sc))
    best8 = global_best.copy()
    sc8 = global_sc

    best16 = None
    t16_start = time.monotonic()
    t16_dead = min(float(t_high16_end), float(deadline) - 150.0)
    if t16_dead - t16_start > 150.0:
        sc16, p16 = refine_candidate(global_best, 16384, t16_dead, "high16", rng)
        best16 = p16
        if np.isfinite(sc16) and sc16 < global_sc:
            global_sc = float(sc16)
            global_best = p16.copy()
            print("high16 improved score", float(global_sc))
        else:
            print("high16 best score", float(sc16) if np.isfinite(sc16) else "inf")
    else:
        print("skipping high16 due to time")

    if best16 is not None:
        final_pool = [global_best, best8, best16]
    else:
        final_pool = [global_best, best8]

    final_best = None
    final_sc = np.inf
    for arr in final_pool:
        if arr is None:
            continue
        ar = normalize_a(arr)
        if ar.size == 0:
            continue
        sc = score_norm(ar)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < final_sc:
            final_sc = float(sc)
            final_best = ar.copy()

    if final_best is None:
        final_best = normalize_a(resample_sequence(high_starts[0], n8))
        final_sc = score_norm(final_best)

    print("selected final internal score", float(final_sc), "len", final_best.size)

    if time.monotonic() < float(final_dead) - 10.0:
        sc, p2 = log_mirror_search(
            final_best,
            float(final_dead) - 10.0,
            rng,
            alpha_values=(0.5, 1.0, 3.0, 10.0, 30.0),
            eta_values=(0.1, 0.3, 0.8, 1.5),
            max_outer=8000,
        )
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < final_sc:
            final_sc = float(sc)
            final_best = p2.copy()
            print("final mirror polish", float(final_sc))

    if time.monotonic() < float(final_dead) - 35.0 and final_best.size <= 8192:
        sc, p2 = run_y_lbfgs(final_best, 60.0 * float(final_best.size), float(final_dead) - 35.0, 500, 1e-6)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < final_sc:
            final_sc = float(sc)
            final_best = p2.copy()
            print("final y polish", float(final_sc))

    out_arr = normalize_a(final_best)
    if out_arr.size == 0:
        out_arr = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0
    out_arr = np.nan_to_num(out_arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    out_arr = np.clip(out_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    S = float(out_arr.sum())
    if S < 0.01:
        out_arr = np.full(1024, 0.5, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        out_arr = out_arr / S
        out_arr = np.clip(out_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)

    out = [float(x) for x in out_arr]
    internal = final_sc
    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
    except Exception:
        verified = internal
    print("FINAL internal", internal, "verified", verified, "len", len(out), "time", time.monotonic() - t_start)
    return out
```
